function NavButton({
  active,
  icon,
  label,
  onClick,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  icon: ReactNode;
  label: string;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-left text-sm font-semibold transition ${
        active
          ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
          : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-50"
      }`}
    >
      {icon}
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}

function StatCard({
  label,
  value,
  icon,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string | number;
  icon: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="agriva-role-stat rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
      <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
        {label}
      </p>

      <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
          {value}
        </p>

        {icon}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function InfoBox({
  label,
  value,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-3">
      <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
        {label}
      </p>

      <p className="mt-1 break-words text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">
        {value}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function ManagementSection({
  title,
  subtitle,
  children,
}: {
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900">
          {title}
        </h2>

        <p className="mt-1 text-slate-500">
          {subtitle}
        </p>
      </div>

      {children}
    </>
  );
}

function StatusBadge({
  status,
}: {
  status: string;
}) {
  const normalized =
    status.toLowerCase();

  const className =
    normalized === "active" ||
    normalized === "paid" ||
    normalized === "completed"
      ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"
      : normalized === "accepted"
        ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700"
        : normalized === "refunded"
          ? "bg-violet-100 text-violet-700"
          : normalized === "cancelled" ||
              normalized === "rejected" ||
              normalized === "failed"
            ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
            : "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700";

  return (
    <span
      className={`inline-flex rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold capitalize ${className}`}
    >
      {status.replaceAll("_", " ")}
    </span>
  );
}

function RoleBadge({
  role,
}: {
  role: string;
}) {
  const className =
    role === "ADMIN"
      ? "bg-purple-100 text-purple-700"
      : role === "FARMER"
        ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"
        : "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700";

  return (
    <span
      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold ${className}`}
    >
      {role}
    </span>
  );
}

function DataTable({
  children,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
      <table className="w-full min-w-[700px] text-left text-sm">
        {children}
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

function TableHead({
  children,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <th className="border-b pb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
      {children}
    </th>
  );
}

function TableCell({
  children,
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <td className="py-4 text-slate-700">
      {children}
    </td>
  );
}

function EmptyState({
  icon,
  title,
  message,
}: {
  icon: ReactNode;
  title: string;
  message: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-10 text-center shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex justify-center text-slate-300">
        {icon}
      </div>

      <h3 className="mt-4 font-bold text-slate-800">
        {title}
      </h3>

      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
        {message}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function isRecord(
  value: unknown
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function toText(
  value: unknown,
  fallback = ""
): string {
  if (typeof value === "string") {
    const trimmed = value.trim();
    return trimmed || fallback;
  }

  if (
    typeof value === "number" ||
    typeof value === "boolean"
  ) {
    return String(value);
  }

  return fallback;
}

function toNullableText(
  value: unknown
): string | null {
  const text = toText(value);
  return text || null;
}

function toBoolean(
  value: unknown,
  fallback = false
): boolean {
  if (typeof value === "boolean") {
    return value;
  }

  if (typeof value === "number") {
    return value !== 0;
  }

  if (typeof value === "string") {
    const normalized =
      value.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (
      normalized === "true" ||
      normalized === "1" ||
      normalized === "yes"
    ) {
      return true;
    }

    if (
      normalized === "false" ||
      normalized === "0" ||
      normalized === "no"
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return fallback;
}

function toStringArray(
  value: unknown
): string[] | null {
  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
    return null;
  }

  return value
    .map((item) => toText(item))
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function extractArray<T>(
  value: unknown,
  keys: string[]
): T[] {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return value as T[];
  }

  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return [];
  }

  for (const key of keys) {
    const candidate = value[key];

    if (Array.isArray(candidate)) {
      return candidate as T[];
    }
  }

  return [];
}

function toNumber(
  value: unknown,
  fallback = 0
) {
  if (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value)
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  if (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    value.trim() !== ""
  ) {
    const parsed = Number(value);

    if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
      return parsed;
    }
  }

  return fallback;
}

function toOptionalNumber(
  value: unknown
): number | null {
  if (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value)
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  if (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    value.trim() !== ""
  ) {
    const parsed = Number(value);

    if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) {
      return parsed;
    }
  }

  return null;
}

function formatOptionalCount(
  value: number | null | undefined
) {
  return value == null
    ? "—"
    : String(value);
}

function formatDate(
  value: string
) {
  const date = new Date(value);

  if (
    Number.isNaN(date.getTime())
  ) {
    return value;
  }

  return date.toLocaleString(
    "en-IN",
    {
      dateStyle: "medium",
      timeStyle: "short",
    }
  );
}


=================================================
FILE: .\app\login\page.tsx
=================================================
"use client";
import Link from "next/link";
import {
  FormEvent,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Leaf,
  Loader2,
  Lock,
  Mail,
  ShieldCheck,
  ShoppingBag,
  Sprout,
} from "lucide-react";

import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";

import {
  useLocale,
  useTranslations,
} from "next-intl";

import LanguageSelector from "@/components/LanguageSelector";


const API_URL =
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL ||
  "http://127.0.0.1:8000";


type LoginRole =
  | "FARMER"
  | "BUYER"
  | "ADMIN";


const DEMO_ACCOUNTS: Record<
  LoginRole,
  {
    email: string;
    password: string;
  }
> = {
  FARMER: {
    email: "farmer@agriva.demo",
    password: "Farmer@123",
  },

  BUYER: {
    email: "buyer@agriva.demo",
    password: "Buyer@123",
  },

  ADMIN: {
    email: "admin@agriva.demo",
    password: "Admin@123",
  },
};


export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const t = useTranslations();
  const locale = useLocale();

  const [selectedRole, setSelectedRole] =
    useState<LoginRole>("FARMER");

  const [email, setEmail] =
    useState(
      DEMO_ACCOUNTS.FARMER.email
    );

  const [password, setPassword] =
    useState(
      DEMO_ACCOUNTS.FARMER.password
    );

  const [
    showPassword,
    setShowPassword,
  ] = useState(false);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(false);

  const [error, setError] =
    useState("");


  const isHindi =
    locale === "hi";


  function ui(
    english: string,
    hindi: string
  ) {
    return isHindi
      ? hindi
      : english;
  }


  function selectRole(
    role: LoginRole
  ) {
    setSelectedRole(role);

    setEmail(
      DEMO_ACCOUNTS[role].email
    );

    setPassword(
      DEMO_ACCOUNTS[role].password
    );

    setError("");
  }


  async function handleLogin(
    event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>
  ) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      localStorage.removeItem(
        "access_token"
      );

      localStorage.removeItem(
        "refresh_token"
      );

      localStorage.removeItem(
        "token"
      );

      const response = await fetch(
        `${API_URL}/api/auth/login`,
        {
          method: "POST",

          headers: {
            "Content-Type":
              "application/json",
          },

          body: JSON.stringify({
            email: email.trim(),
            password,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await response
        .json()
        .catch(() => null);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.detail ||
            t(
              "errors.loginFailed"
            )
        );
      }

      if (!data?.access_token) {
        throw new Error(
          t(
            "login.accessTokenMissing"
          )
        );
      }

      localStorage.setItem(
        "access_token",
        data.access_token
      );

      if (data.refresh_token) {
        localStorage.setItem(
          "refresh_token",
          data.refresh_token
        );
      }

      /*
       * Important:
       * The selected role is only a login-page convenience.
       * AGRIVA always trusts the authenticated role returned
       * by /api/auth/me for authorization and redirection.
       */
      const meResponse =
        await fetch(
          `${API_URL}/api/auth/me`,
          {
            method: "GET",

            headers: {
              Authorization:
                `Bearer ${data.access_token}`,
            },

            cache: "no-store",
          }
        );

      const user =
        await meResponse
          .json()
          .catch(() => null);

      if (!meResponse.ok) {
        localStorage.removeItem(
          "access_token"
        );

        localStorage.removeItem(
          "refresh_token"
        );

        throw new Error(
          user?.detail ||
            t(
              "login.userInfoFailed"
            )
        );
      }

      const role = String(
        user?.role || ""
      )
        .trim()
        .toUpperCase();

      if (role === "FARMER") {
        router.replace(
          "/dashboard/farmer"
        );

        return;
      }

      if (role === "BUYER") {
        router.replace(
          "/dashboard/buyer"
        );

        return;
      }

      if (role === "ADMIN") {
        router.replace(
          "/dashboard/admin"
        );

        return;
      }

      localStorage.removeItem(
        "access_token"
      );

      localStorage.removeItem(
        "refresh_token"
      );

      throw new Error(
        `${t(
          "login.unknownRole"
        )}: ${
          role ||
          t(
            "login.missingRole"
          )
        }`
      );
    } catch (err) {
      localStorage.removeItem(
        "access_token"
      );

      localStorage.removeItem(
        "refresh_token"
      );

      localStorage.removeItem(
        "token"
      );

      setError(
        err instanceof Error
          ? err.message
          : t(
              "errors.loginFailed"
            )
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }


  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 px-4 py-8 sm:py-10">
      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-5xl">

        {/* LANGUAGE */}

        <div className="mb-5 flex justify-end">
          <LanguageSelector />
        </div>


        {/* BRAND */}

        <div className="mb-7 text-center">
          <Link
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-3xl font-black text-emerald-700"
          >
            <Leaf size={32} />

            {t(
              "common.appName"
            )}
          </Link>

          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
            {t(
              "login.tagline"
            )}
          </p>
        </div>


        <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1.05fr]">

          {/* ROLE PANEL */}

          <section className="rounded-3xl bg-emerald-700 p-6 text-white shadow-xl sm:p-8">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-100">
              {ui(
                "Choose your role",
                "अपनी भूमिका चुनें"
              )}
            </p>

            <h2 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-black">
              {ui(
                "Continue to AGRIVA",
                "AGRIVA में आगे बढ़ें"
              )}
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-emerald-100">
              {ui(
                "Select Farmer, Buyer or Admin. For demo testing, the matching credentials are filled automatically.",
                "किसान, खरीदार या एडमिन चुनें। डेमो टेस्टिंग के लिए संबंधित लॉगिन विवरण अपने-आप भर जाएंगे।"
              )}
            </p>

            <div className="mt-7 space-y-3">

              <RoleButton
                active={
                  selectedRole ===
                  "FARMER"
                }
                title={ui(
                  "Farmer",
                  "किसान"
                )}
                subtitle={ui(
                  "Manage crops, AI tools and orders",
                  "फसल, AI टूल और ऑर्डर प्रबंधित करें"
                )}
                icon={
                  <Sprout className="h-6 w-6" />
                }
                onClick={() =>
                  selectRole(
                    "FARMER"
                  )
                }
              />

              <RoleButton
                active={
                  selectedRole ===
                  "BUYER"
                }
                title={ui(
                  "Buyer",
                  "खरीदार"
                )}
                subtitle={ui(
                  "Explore crops and place orders",
                  "फसल देखें और ऑर्डर करें"
                )}
                icon={
                  <ShoppingBag className="h-6 w-6" />
                }
                onClick={() =>
                  selectRole(
                    "BUYER"
                  )
                }
              />

              <RoleButton
                active={
                  selectedRole ===
                  "ADMIN"
                }
                title={ui(
                  "Admin",
                  "एडमिन"
                )}
                subtitle={ui(
                  "Monitor the AGRIVA platform",
                  "AGRIVA प्लेटफ़ॉर्म की निगरानी करें"
                )}
                icon={
                  <ShieldCheck className="h-6 w-6" />
                }
                onClick={() =>
                  selectRole(
                    "ADMIN"
                  )
                }
              />
            </div>

            <div className="mt-7 rounded-2xl border border-white/20 bg-white/10 p-4">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-100">
                {ui(
                  "Selected demo account",
                  "चुना गया डेमो अकाउंट"
                )}
              </p>

              <p className="mt-2 break-all text-sm font-semibold">
                {
                  DEMO_ACCOUNTS[
                    selectedRole
                  ].email
                }
              </p>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-emerald-100">
                {
                  DEMO_ACCOUNTS[
                    selectedRole
                  ].password
                }
              </p>
            </div>
          </section>


          {/* LOGIN CARD */}

          <section className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl sm:p-8">
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
              {t(
                "login.welcomeBack"
              )}
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
              {t(
                "login.subtitle"
              )}
            </p>

            <div className="mt-4 inline-flex rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">
              {selectedRole ===
              "FARMER"
                ? ui(
                    "Farmer Login",
                    "किसान लॉगिन"
                  )
                : selectedRole ===
                  "BUYER"
                ? ui(
                    "Buyer Login",
                    "खरीदार लॉगिन"
                  )
                : ui(
                    "Admin Login",
                    "एडमिन लॉगिन"
                  )}
            </div>


            {/* ERROR */}

            {error && (
              <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-600">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}


            {/* FORM */}

            <form
              onSubmit={
                handleLogin
              }
              className="mt-6 space-y-5"
            >

              {/* EMAIL */}

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {t(
                    "forms.email"
                  )}
                </label>

                <div className="relative">
                  <Mail
                    size={18}
                    className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
                  />

                  <input
                    type="email"
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(
                      event
                    ) =>
                      setEmail(
                        event.target
                          .value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder={t(
                      "login.emailPlaceholder"
                    )}
                    autoComplete="email"
                    required
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 py-3 pl-10 pr-4 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-100"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>


              {/* PASSWORD */}

              <div>
                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">
                  {t(
                    "forms.password"
                  )}
                </label>

                <div className="relative">
                  <Lock
                    size={18}
                    className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400"
                  />

                  <input
                    type={
                      showPassword
                        ? "text"
                        : "password"
                    }
                    value={
                      password
                    }
                    onChange={(
                      event
                    ) =>
                      setPassword(
                        event.target
                          .value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder={t(
                      "login.passwordPlaceholder"
                    )}
                    autoComplete="current-password"
                    required
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 py-3 pl-10 pr-12 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-emerald-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-emerald-100"
                  />

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() =>
                      setShowPassword(
                        (
                          current
                        ) =>
                          !current
                      )
                    }
                    className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-md p-1 text-slate-400 transition hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
                    aria-label={
                      showPassword
                        ? ui(
                            "Hide password",
                            "पासवर्ड छिपाएं"
                          )
                        : ui(
                            "Show password",
                            "पासवर्ड दिखाएं"
                          )
                    }
                  >
                    {showPassword ? (
                      <EyeOff
                        size={18}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <Eye
                        size={18}
                      />
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>


              {/* LOGIN */}

              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 py-3 font-semibold text-white transition hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
              >
                {loading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2
                      size={18}
                      className="animate-spin"
                    />

                    {t(
                      "login.loggingIn"
                    )}
                  </>
                ) : (
                  t(
                    "login.login"
                  )
                )}
              </button>
            </form>


            <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              {t(
                "login.noAccount"
              )}{" "}

              <a
                href="/register"
                className="font-semibold text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700"
              >
                {t(
                  "login.createAccount"
                )}
              </a>
            </p>


            <div className="mt-6 border-t border-slate-100 pt-5">
              <p className="text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
                {ui(
                  "The selected role never bypasses security. AGRIVA redirects using the authenticated role returned by the backend.",
                  "चुनी गई भूमिका सुरक्षा को बायपास नहीं करती। AGRIVA बैकएंड से प्राप्त वास्तविक भूमिका के अनुसार ही रीडायरेक्ट करता है।"
                )}
              </p>
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}


function RoleButton({
  active,
  title,
  subtitle,
  icon,
  onClick,
}: {
  active: boolean;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  onClick: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`flex w-full items-center gap-4 rounded-2xl border p-4 text-left transition ${
        active
          ? "border-white bg-white text-emerald-800 shadow-lg"
          : "border-white/20 bg-white/10 text-white hover:bg-white/15"
      }`}
    >
      <div
        className={`rounded-xl p-3 ${
          active
            ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700"
            : "bg-white/10 text-white"
        }`}
      >
        {icon}
      </div>

      <div>
        <p className="font-bold">
          {title}
        </p>

        <p
          className={`mt-1 text-xs ${
            active
              ? "text-slate-500"
              : "text-emerald-100"
          }`}
        >
          {subtitle}
        </p>
      </div>
    </button>
  );
}



=================================================
FILE: .\components\language\LanguageSwitcher.tsx
=================================================
"use client";

import { Languages } from "lucide-react";
import { useLocale, useTranslations } from "next-intl";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect } from "react";

type Locale = "mr" | "hi" | "en";

const STORAGE_KEY = "agriva-language";
const COOKIE_KEY = "AGRIVA_LOCALE";
const SUPPORTED_LOCALES: Locale[] = ["mr", "hi", "en"];

function isSupportedLocale(value: string | null): value is Locale {
  return value !== null && SUPPORTED_LOCALES.includes(value as Locale);
}

function writeLocaleCookie(locale: Locale) {
  document.cookie = `${COOKIE_KEY}=${locale}; path=/; max-age=31536000; SameSite=Lax`;
}

export default function LanguageSwitcher() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const locale = useLocale() as Locale;
  const t = useTranslations("common");

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const savedLocale = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);

      if (isSupportedLocale(savedLocale)) {
        if (savedLocale !== locale) {
          writeLocaleCookie(savedLocale);
          router.refresh();
        }
        return;
      }

      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, locale);
      writeLocaleCookie(locale);
    } catch {
      // Cookie-based locale still works when localStorage is unavailable.
    }
  }, [locale, router]);

  function changeLanguage(nextLocale: Locale) {
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, nextLocale);
    } catch {
      // Keep language switching functional even if storage is blocked.
    }

    writeLocaleCookie(nextLocale);
    router.refresh();
  }

  return (
    <label className="agriva-language-switcher inline-flex min-h-12 items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-slate-700">
      <Languages className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-green-700" aria-hidden="true" />
      <span className="sr-only">{t("selectLanguage")}</span>
      <select
        value={locale}
        onChange={(event) => changeLanguage(event.target.value as Locale)}
        className="min-w-0 bg-transparent text-base font-semibold outline-none"
        aria-label={t("selectLanguage")}
      >
        <option value="mr">मराठी</option>
        <option value="hi">हिंदी</option>
        <option value="en">English</option>
      </select>
    </label>
  );
}
